# Self-hosted Google Fonts — Licenses & Attributions

The `.woff2` files in this directory are redistributed from the upstream
[google/fonts](https://github.com/google/fonts) repository. Each family is licensed
under one of the open-source licenses listed below; the full license texts are
reproduced at the end of this file as required by their terms.

This file is generated by `scripts/fetch-licenses.js` and refreshed by the weekly
Google Fonts auto-update workflow. Do not edit by hand.
